*It was amazing and loving reading all your comments, I saw, I read and I'm impressed. Know that I'm happy. Yours Truly Antyn Hikmah*



Wannan furucin nashi Ya girgiza min duniyata da abinda ke cikinta. Bansan yadda zan kwatanta muku yadda sakonshi ya iso gareni ba, one thing shi ne daga ranar komai nawa Ya canza. Nasan da yawa zakuce Hamma bai kyauta ba, zakuce he messed up! Yeah Hakane amma abu daya da yasa ya fada min hakan, saboda ganin Bashida wata mafita data rage mishi banda hakan, yana ganin zai rasa ni matsawar bai fada min ba. 

Kallonshi nayi Kamar yadda yake kallona, idanunshi Sunyi tas sede akwai Dana sani a cikinsu, Wanda I'm sure Dan Ya fada min abinda Baffa da Mama sukai ta harkilon rufe min ne, amma aikin gama ya gama. 

"Hamma zan fita"

Na fada can Kasan makoshina, idanuna Sunyi jawur Ko ban Kalli madubi ba, nasan da hakan Dan har wani yaji yaji nakeji. Bai Musa min ba ya bude min na fito, ina jinshi yana fadin 

"Allah Ya tashe mu lafiya"

Ko amsa ban bashi ba na wuce cikin gida. A hankali na murda kofar parlor, cikin zuciyata addua nake Allah yasa Mama da Baffa basu parlon. Amma ina sallama wadda a hankali ta fita saboda yadda muryar take a shake, babu wani kuzari Haka na shiga na sameshi suna zaune da alamu zamansu kenan. 

"Baffa, hannu iwar tauki, Mama miwarti"

Na fada Ina daidaita yanayina, amma banyi nasara ba, Mama ta zuba min idanu, yayinda Baffa yace

"Uwata me ya sameki? Kinga idonki kuwa? "

A hankali na lumshe idon, Karo na farko kwakwalwar Kaina tace Basu suka haifeki ba, idanuna na bude ina karkaro murmushin da iyakar kokarina amma fatar baki kawai ya tsaya nace. 

"Abu ya fada min a ido. Mama zan kwanta kowa na gaisheki"

Ina fadar Haka na juya da sauri, saboda Ina tsoron tambayoyi, bana son a tambayeni Dan banida amsar basu, na nufi dakina inajin Baffa na cewa Mama kada ta bini ta barni tukun. Nikam Ina shiga dakina, na tura kofar na jingina da ita ina jin yadda zuciyata ke bugawa Kamar zata fasa kirjina ta fito, ga numfashina da kyar nake iya janyo shi. There and then nasan attack zan samu, hakan yasa nayi saurin saka lock a kofar, drawer naje na ciro inhaler babu jira na dinga shakawa, hawaye ya wanke fuskata tamkar zanyi hauka haka na dinga ji a wannan lokacin, saboda naki na nutsu shiyasa asthma taki sauka Se karuwa abin yake, ganin da gaske idan na cigaba da zama to tabbas lungs dina collapsing zasui saboda da kyar nake fuzgar iska Ko kuma na mutu ban su waye suka haifeni ba, bansan ko ni Shegiya ce ba.

Mikewa nayi ilahirin jikina karkarwa yake na bude kofar na fito parlor, tunkan na karaso naji Salatin Mama daga nan ban kara sanin inda nake ba, Se farkawa nayi naga oxygen a hancina a gadon asibiti. 

Hawaye a hankali ya Fara sauka saman kuncina, idanuna na runtse inajin wani abu me tsini yana piercing kirjin, ga Kaina tamkar zai fado haka nakeji. A hankali naji Mama ta kama hannuna tana fadin 

"Hikmah kin tashi"

Idanuna na bude, hakan yasa ta tabbatar da zarginta, hannuna ta sake min ta fita, can Sega ta ta dawo da nurse, tana zuwa ta ciremin oxygen din sannan tace

"Zainab tashi ki zauna"

A hankali na tashi na zauna har lokacin kuka nake, Wanda hawaye ne kawai se wani tukukin takaici da bakin ciki marar misaltuwa da nake ji cikin zuciyata. 

"Ya jikin? "

Ta tambaya tana hada allura cikin syringe, kaina na gyada mata Dan Ko bakina bana son budewa. Anan tamin allura wadda ta kusa mintina ashirin tanayi, tana gamawa ta dubeni tace 

"Me yake miki ciwo? "

Kaina na girgiza nace 

"kaina da kirjima"

Fita Tayi can se gata ta dawo dauke da abin BP, tana gwadawa naga ta Kara Har sau uku sannan tace

"Ke me yake damunki, me yasa BPnki Zaiyi shooting haka"

Wannan me yake damunki da tace se na fashe da kuka me ciwo, me zance? Basu bane iyaye na? Waye ya haifeni? Kaina na girgiza Ina kankame jikina, ko mintina basuyi ba asthma ta Kara tashi. Nan da nan aka bani taimakon gaggawa sannan akai min allurar bacci Dan na huta. 

Seda nayi kwana biyar a asibiti sannan aka sallame mu, tunda nake ban taba ganin Tashin hankali irin wannan da na shiga ba a wannan lokacin ba, Ko a mafarki ban taba tunanin akwai irin wannan yanayin ba. Seda naji komai da kowa ya fita akaina. Nasha Yan dubiya amma duk idan na tuna cewar Kila babu Hadin iya balle na Baba Se sabon kuka. Mama tayi juyin duniya na fada mata abinda yake damuna amma sede kuka, ko kuma idan taci saa nace Babu komai Kaina na kawai. Baffa Dama nasiha kawai yake min. Su Anty Fadila kuwa sede kallo da ido. Ranar da Hamma Mussadiq yazo dubani babu kowa a dakin Se ni kadai na idar da sallah ina karatun Qurani ya shigo, inajinshi ya zauna amma naki dagowa balle na kalleshi, kuma tunda ya shigo na daina fahimtar Harrufan  balle har na hada karatun ya tashi

"Hikmah kiyi hakuri, ban taba kawowa idan na fada miki hakan zaki damu kanki ba. Ba se kinyi hakan ba, ba se Kin sawa Kanki hawan jini ba kina neman kashe Kanki, simple Hamma Bazan aureka ba ya wadatar Hikma. Just ki Kalli madubi kiga yadda kika koma..... "

Ban barshi ya karasa fadar rubbish dinsa ba na katse shi ina fadin 

"Me Kake tunani? Saboda kace kana sona zanyiwa kaina Haka? My friend stop dreaming please. Ka manta? Kodan ka manta, amma ina son Kasan ka barmin tabon da har abada bazai goge ba. Nagode sosae da ka farkar Dani daga baccin da nake, Nagode da ka fadamin Baffa da Mama ba iyayena bane. "

Ina gama fadar Haka na Mike na nufi kofa, ban bude ba amma na tsaya nace

"Hamma zo ka fita, bana son ganinka!"

Babu musu sede jikinshi yayi mugun sanyi yazo Ya wuce ya fita, da gudu na fada gado na rushe da kuka. Tundaga ranar Har aka sallemeni bai Kara dawowa ba. Muna zuwa gida Mama duk ta kora su Aneeysa gidajensu Dan tunda na kwanta suke jele a gurina. Dakina na wuce bisa umarnin Mama akan nayi wanka na canja kaya kafin nan ta gama Abinci. Dakina na wuce bayan Munyi sallama dasu Anty Fadila, ni Kaina kyankyamin kaina nake saboda I hate asibiti abinda ya hanani yin clinicals kenan, Bazan iya coping warin Izal, Hypo, Jik dasu Sevulum ba. 

Ina shiga naji tamkar Bakuwa ba Kamar yadda na saba ji idan na dawo dakin bayan doguwar tafiya, mostly da na dawo zan fada gadona na kankame duvet cover din amma Yanzun se naji bai birgeni, wanka nayi Nazo na Nemo saukakkiyar Abaya red na saka, mirror naje na kalla seda na tsorata tsabar yadda na rame, idanuna da mutane ke fadar su, sun zurma ciki ga bags a Kasan su, wannan dogon hancin nawa Ya fito yayi tsaho saboda duk kumatun sun sabe Se zygomatic kadai. Wuyana kam Baa magana Dan kashin nan sun fito Sunyi kwal tamkar a aje abu su zauna. Hawaye a hankali ya gangaro min nasa hannuna na goge sannan na fita jin Mama ta Fara kwalla min kira. 

A parlor na sameta da bowl cikeda fruit salad, ina zama ta miko min, karba Nayi nasha dukda taste din was heaven amma Bazan iya ba gaba daya hankalina baya kan Abinci duk yadda nakai da sonshi. 

"Hikmah"

Dagowa nayi na dubi Mama nace

"Naam Mama"

"Duk duniyar nan Kinada Wanda yafini? Kin canza iyaye ne? "

Da sauri na shiga girgiza Kaina ina share hawayen da ya Fara bayyana a kumatuna, nace

"A'a Mama"

Amma cikin zuciyata wani girma da soyayyar Maman Ya kara cikani sede Bazan iya fada mata danuwata ba

"Me yake damunki? Ki fada min Kinji. Ko Bazan miki magani ba amma nasan zakiji sauki a ranki. "

Kaina na girgiza zuwa lokacin kuka nake sosae nace

"Mama kiyi hakuri kawai kiyi min addua"

Abu daya da Mama batada takura bazata taba takura maka ba, Tundaga lokacin bata kara Maganar ba sede tace Allah ya saukaka miki. 

Se dare na nemi wayata, message na Fara budewa Naga messages din Hamma Aiman rututu, hawaye sosae Ya kwance min, duk wannan abin we're not related in anyway. Se kuma wata bakuwar glo number da tayi min message. Ban bude ba na Fara kiran Hamma Aiman, bugu daya Ya katse ya kirani yana fadin 

"Kanwata ina kika shiga? "

Kunsan akwai mutanen da naturally kana cikin damuwa, Ko hakuri suka Baka Se kayi kuka, take kuwa na Fara kuka amma bai katseni ba seda na gama sannan yace 

"Tell me, me ya faru"

Babu abinda na boye mishi na fada masa komai sannan nace

"Dama na dade ina tunanin Anya gidanmu nake zaune? Babu wani abu da zai nuna maka sudin Yan uwana ne..... "

Se kuka ya hanani karasawa hakan yasa nayi iya yina sannan yace

"Naji komai Hikmah, I'm very very sorry. Banso Haka labarin ya sameki ba. Amfanin boye zancennan ya Kare Dan tabbas Hakane abinda hammanki ya fada. Sede ke me kike gani? "

Idanuna na goge nace

"Babu abinda zan fadawa kowa, nasan tunda na sani iyayena duk inda suke zan gansu, kuma indai Munada rabo Haduwa zamu hadu dasu. Amma Bazan iya tunkarar Baffa da Mama da wannan zancen ba, they've done alot "

Aiman yaji dadin abinda na fada, ina tunanin ya kamata a dauki Aiman a matsayin lecturer, Ya iya bada lecture, komai da ilimi yake yinshi. Seda yayi min nasiha sosae, ya bani baki sannan muka rabu.

*There's suspected and confirmed case of Lassa fever in some states across the federation. Let's all take a moment and pray for those that are infected, in isolation and those that are already late. To we that are solidly OK, fit and fine. I pray ya Hayyu ya Kayyum continue to protect us from every disease, May Allah Azzawajal ease all of our worries, May he guide us and light our path. Know that prevention is far more better than curing. Let's all be vigilant cuz health is wealth, Beware of Rotten Tomatoes bcux you don't know which rat fed on it. You can't be following rats to see whether it's multi mammate or has a single breast. Is not easy my dear, be wise hand washing is everything, use salt in washing vegetables and fruits! And also High grade fever, sore throat, bleeding from body Orrifices, vomiting, diarrhoea are some cardinal signs. Report ASAP! May Allah almighty make it easy for us, may he guide us through all our daily endeavors!*

*OCT, 1997*


_Ranar ta kasance jumaa, Baffa da iyalanshi Mama, Musbahu, Mussadiq, Mustapha da autan Mama na gaban goshi Mus'ab. Dukkansu suna cikin motarsu sun dawo daga Samunaka kaduna. Sunje ziyarar wani Yayan Baffa Wanda shi ne Mahaifin Abba (Mr perfect). Kana kallonsu Kasan family ne Wanda zaman lafiya, kauna da shakikiyar soyayya take wakana a ciki. Sunyi nisa sosae har suka shigo wani karamin gari na doguwa, anan Baffa ya tsaya ya siya musu tsire da juices, Mus'ab kasancewarsa shi ne karami aka siyo masa milky juice. Suka cigaba da tafiya cikeda farin ciki a Haka Mustapha da Mus'ab dake back seat sukai bacci._

_A hankali Baffa ya Fara rage gudun motar da yake, Ya dan juyo kadan yace_

_Kowa Yayi addua zamu shiga Falgore game reserve_

_Kai dukkansu suka gyada dan lokacin, hanyar batada kyau hardly ayi sati daya ba tareda record din gagarumin robbery a hanyar ba. Shiyasa mutane ke fargabar wucewa ta gurin. Haka suka dinga tafiya babu wata Mota Se kwaya biyu da suka wuce. Seda sukaje tsakiyar hanyar sannan Wasu mutane kowanne rikeda bindigogi, ga mask a fuskokinsu sanye da bakaken kaya. Suka bayyana a tsakiyar titin._

_Tsabar yadda Baffa ya gigice, rikicewa Yayi yana neman sakin steering, Mama hannu ta daura akai tana Salati, sukam su Musbahu karar brake din da Baffa ya taka, yasa duk suka dawo daga abinda sukeyi, Mus'ab ganin an bude kofar an fita da Baffa da Mama, yasa ya Fara kuka. Duk wani abu dake jikin bayin Allah nan biyu babu abinda yake banda rawa, tsabar firgita, Mama se kuka takeyi tana rokonsu su kyalesu amma ko sauraronta basuyi, ga babu wata Mota da take da alamar zata wuce saboda yadda mutane suke very wary of hanyar._

_"Malam dago muyi magana ta fahimta, Dan nan da kake ganinmu bawai fashi ko wani abu zamu yi muku ba"_

_Jin wannan furuci daga bakin shugabansu yasa Baffa ya daidaita kanshi, Mama ta rage sautin kukanta. Ganin Haka na Ogan yayi waving hannunshi, nan take sega yarinya an fito da ita daga jeji, hannunta dauke da biscuits tana kaiwa baki. _

_Ogan ne ya karbe ta, sannan ya mikawa Mama ita yace_

_"Hajiya, wannan yarinyar da kike gani bata san kowa ba, bata san komai ba. Mun taho daga Abuja zuwa jos shekaranjiya a motar mu da muka hau, mukaji Wasu Christians suna zancen sato ta daga gidansu zasu kaiwa dodon tsafi. Yarinyar kana kallonta Kasan yar musulmai ce, Dan Haka mukai ta binsu, cikin dare muka sace ta. Dukkanmu da kuke gani kowannenmu babu me iyali balle ya kai gidanshi, shiyasa muka yanke hunkucin tare hanya mu bada amanarta. Idan da akwai rabo Allah zai hadata da iyayenta, idan babu kuma Allah shiyasan me ya hukunta akanta. Kun amince zaku rike ta? "_

_Cikin sauri Mama ta gyada kanta, Baffa bakinshi Har hardewa yake wajen fadin_

_"Nayi muku alkawarin zan riketa matsawar raina, zan kula da ita Har Allah Ya nuna ikonshi a kanta. Ku kuma Allah Ya duba lamuranku"_

_Daga Haka Kamar yadda sukai alkawari basui musu komai ba, suka sallamesu, Mama kankame da Lulu a hannunta. Kayan da Hafsa ta saka mata Tun na birthday Har lokacin kwana uku sune a jikinta Baa canja mata ba. Hatta bracelets da tag din dake hannunta Cynthia Batayi nutsuwar cireshi ba. Baki daya Mama take ta bin Lulu da kallo Wadda jinta jikin Mama yasa tayi bacci, kana kallonta Kasan yar gata ce, ko suwaye iyayenta suna ji da ita, she just look like a princess. Shiru ko kwakkwaran motsi babu cikin motar se wani uban gudu da Baffa ke shararawa. Seda ya shigo cikin garin Kano sannan ya daidaita gudun amma har lokacin jikinshu bai bar Bari ba._

_Misalin uku na yamma suka karaso gidansu, hankalin su dukka Baa kwance yake ba. Baffa yana sauke su Ya juya, itakam Mama da Yaran ciki suka shiga, Har lokacin Lulu bacci take bata farka ba. A daki ta ajeta kan gado sannan ta fito dan gyara gidan. Su Musbahu dukkansu a zazzaune suke, jikinsu Yayi bala'in sanyi, to wannan Tashin hankali har ina? A tare ka a Baka Da Dan ma dan mutum, AI abin se addua kawai, gashi Bakada halin cewar bazaka amsa ba. Kafin dawowar Baffa Mama da taimakon su Musbahu duk ta gama abinda ya dace, ruwa ta tafasa lokacin Lulu ta farka sede Ko uhm Batayi ba balle kuka, hannunta take yawan sawa a baki alamar Tana jin Yunwa. Wanka Mama tayi mata sosae sannan ta goyata tunda batada kayan da zata saka mata. Nan da nan wani baccin ya Kara dauke ta Ko boiled egg and boiled Irish da Mama tayi mata mashing ba taci ba. Har Baffa ya dawo tana goye bayan Mama, seda yayi wanka ya shirya Sannnan ya bawa Mussadiq key akan ya shigo da kayan dake booth. Siyayya ce sosae yayi ma Lulu Tundaga papers har bargo, tamkar yar cikinshi._

_"Amina, Ya kike ganin zamuyi da yarinyar nan?"_

_Baffa ya tambaya yana karbar Lulu wadda aka shiryata cikin below elbow length riga me kyau Ash da yarfin yellow, rigar tayi mata kyau, gashi an bata Abinci duk ta wartsake ba Kamar lokacinda aka basu ita ba._

_"Duk yadda kace Baffan Musbahu hakan nan zaayi, amma Nidai har zuciyata inason yarinyar, Ina ganin mu riketa kawai"_

_Cikeda jin dadi Baffa ya murmursa, Wanda rabon da yayi Murmushi Tun kafin suje Falgore. Hannun Mama Ya kama yace_

_"Allah Yayi miki albarka Amina, Inshaaa Allah wannan yarinyar se ta zame mana hasken idaniya"_

_"Amin Amin"_

_Mama ta ansa mishi. Washegari suka shirya duka suka tafi Barikin ladi, inda Baffa ya kaiwa iyayenshi Zainab wadda dama sunan mahaifiyarshi ne, kuma Haka ya gani jikin bracelet din hannunta. Mahaifinshi bai wani damu sosae ba yace Da ai na kowa ne, bakasan Wanda zaka amfana dashi ba, sosae Ya sakawa Hikmah albarka Kamar yadda Mussadiq ya rada mata. Yan Uwa na kusa duk sun goyi bayan rikon Se kadan da suke ganin Ko annoba Muhammadu Ya jajibo musu. Haka suka karasa garinsu Mama Mangu nan ma duk daya, dake da ne duniyar a kwance take, kowa so yake yayi taimako ba Yanzu da kowa ke tsoron kowa ba._

_Cikeda nasara suka dawo Kano, inda kwanansu biyu suka koma sabuwar anguwa, nan sunje da Hikmah, babu Wanda zai kawo cewar ba yarsu bace. Seda sukai sati uku sannan Baffa ya tara duka Yayansa hudun yayi musu warning me karfin gaske akan Hikma_

_"Nasan Dukkanku kunsan yadda akai muka samu Hikma. Nasan ko Mus'ab bazai manta ba. Inason ku tayani rike Amana, ku Tayani jaddadda Ikon Allah, ku rike ta a matsayin yar Uwa wadda muka Haifa, ku goge hoton scenario tsintar ta a brains dinku, ku dauka cewar ita din jininku ce. Dan duk Wanda ya Bari Hikmah tasan matsayinta a gurinshi tabbas zai hadu da fushina. Ni na haramta aure tsakaninku saboda matsawar wannan ya shigo to se na fadawa Hikmah ita ba yata bace. Ina fatan kowa zai kiyaye. "_

_Tundaga wannan ranar suka cigaba da rayuwarsu happily, mutane da yawa idan suka hadu da Mama zasu tambayeta yaushe kika haihu babu sanarwa sede tayi dariya ta bada hakuri. Kowa a gidannan San Hikmah yake, wannan ya dauketa wannan ya ajeta. DUK Wanda Ya fita to kuwa indai zai dawo se ya kawowa Hikmah abu. Wannan shi ne Farkon dasa muguwar kiyayyar ta a Zuciyar Mus'ab, saboda shi ne auta duk attention din kowa akanshi yake amma zuwan Hikmah kidan se ya canja salo. Ya zalinci Hikmah yadda baku zato gashi seda tayi shekara biyu da watanni sannan ta Fara tafiya, anan aka fahimci matsalar da ta sameta Tun a ciki ta kafarta ta hagu._

_Baffa da Mama Sunyi kokarin na gaske na cika alkawarin da suka dauka. Kayan da suka cirewa Hikmah a zuwanta Mama ta adana with the hope ya zama abinda zai sadata da iyayenta! _

"Ummiey Zainab ita ce sirikar ki, aurenta nake Sonyi saboda Ina sonta"

A matukar razane idanuna a warwaje na kalleshi, sannan a tsorace na Kalli Mrs Hassanah wadda banda Murmushi babu abinda take irin na farin ciki dinnan, Juyawa nayi cikin sauri Kamar zan hantsila na bar gurin, ina jinshi yana kwalla min kira amma ko waiwayowa banyi ba. Sauri nake sosae zuciyata Kamar zata balla kirjina saboda yadda take bugu, idanuna na runtse sannan na bude inajin yadda hawaye ya Fara min tsartuwa. 

Acan Ina barin gurin Abdallah ya biyo bayana amma Umminshi ta riko hannunshi, cikin wani yanayi yace

"Ummiey bata sona kenan?"

Ya fada hankalinshi a matukar tashe, hannunsa Ummiey ta rike sosae sannan ta maida shi office din ta samu guri itama ta zauna tace 

"So, da baka fada mata ba? "

Kanshi Ya gyada yace 

"Yanzun nayi niyyar fada mata kuma kawai se naga tunda kina nan Bari ayi a gabanki"

Murmushi tayi tace 

"Idan ta yarda da kai ta amince to albishir me karfi na farin ciki, Kayi saa kayi dace. Amma ya zakayi da Maganar Mupheeyda?"

Kanshi Ya shafa fuskarshi tayi ja saboda tsabar Tashin hankalin da yake ciki, shi a duniyarshi ya manta da zancen wata Mupheeyda. Mikewa Ummiey tayi tace 

"Idan kayi tunani I'm readily available for you Son "

Daga Haka ta fice ta barshi ya rasa abinda yake mishi dadi, Ya rasa inda zai saka ranshi. 

Ina zuwa daki na wullar da kayan hannuna na fada kan katifata Se kuka, bansan kukan me nakeyi ba, bansan dalilin kukana ba amma inajin wani bakon yanayi, wani yanayi Wanda bantaba jin irinshi ba. Nafi mintina ashirin a Haka sannan na mike Kamar wata yar Maye har layi nake nayi alwala nayi sallah sannan na koma na kwanta Inata expecting call dinsa Ko message dinsa amma har na gaji da jira na hakura. Da daddare restaurant din da muka hadu jiya na koma with the hope ko zaije amma nafi awanni uku babu ko alamar shi. Take kuwa na koma halinda nake ciki a da.

Wasawasa abin Kamar a film kwatakwata Abdallah bai Kara nemana ba, bantaba jin tashin hankali irin wannan ba a rayuwata, Haka na zauna na cigaba da rayuwata tareda jin komai ya fita a Kaina, naji na tsani dukkan komai tareda kara Jinjina abin maza. 

Da daddare Ina zaune a daki wajen sati uku da faruwar abin, Ina kwance Sega message, cikin sauri na mike tunanina Abdallah ne amma Se naga Nana ce, message ne akan na sameta a dakinta. Rufe littafin nayi na tafi dakinta. Ina sallama naji sheshekar kukanta da sauri na karasa Ina fadin 

"Nana me ya faru? "

Tana ta shesheka tace

"Deeda ne Hikmah, Ya daina kirana, nayi mishi text baya responding, na Kira bai dauka ba baya kuma returning calls din"

Wani kululun bakin ciki ne ya tokare min makoshina, mudai mata bamida wata matsala da ta wuce ta da namiji, kullum mu matsalarmu akan namiji ne. 

"Shine kika zauna kike kuka saboda kanin ubanki ya daina kulaki? Meye na damuwa Nanah."

Tana kuka sosae tace 

"Hikmah bazaki gane yadda nake ji ba, ba zaki gane halinda zuciyata ke ciki ba, tamkar ana babbaka wuta Haka nakeji. Ina sonshi!"

"Kuma dole Ki rabu dashi ba, tunda he's  not attached to iskar da kike Shaka. Zancen Bazan gane halinda nake ciki ba, Nanah da heart break na tashi a cikin zuciyata, a Yanzun da kike ganina, zuciyata is broken and is shattered into thousands of pieces. Amma dole Ki zama strong, rabuwa da Masoyi ba shi ne karshen rayuwa ba. "

"Allah Nanah wallahi na daina soyayya, Bazan kara ba. Maza azzalumai ne, duk yadda nake son Deeda Haka ya rabu Dani."

Shiru nayi kawai Dan bansan me zan kara cewa Nanah ba kuma. Seda tayi kukanta me isarta sannan ta hakura, Inata nuna mata yadda rayuwar take ciki wayata ta Fara ringing, dauka nayi ganin Baffa ne me kiran. Cikin fullanci muka gaisa sannan yace

"Uwata waye Abdallah ne? Kin sanshi? "

Shiru nayi Ina tunanin Abdallah kuma, jin nayi Shiru yasa yace

"Wani lecturer dinku, shekaranjiya yazo da iyayenshi akan suna neman aurenki"

"Aure kuma Baffa? "

Na fada Ina zare idanu, Dan Murmushi yayi na Manya yace

"To Meye a auren"

Kaina na girgiza Kamar yana ganina nace

"Babu komai amma nayi tunanin se na gama school sannan"

Dariya yayi yace 

"Ba haka mukai dake ba Zainab, mun yarda akan da zarar miji yazo zanyi miki aure ko ba Haka ba? "

Shiru nayi Dan nasan Haka mukai dashi, amma Har wani ma Abdallah zaije yayimin Haka, yabi ta sama. Kenan ba rabuwa yayi Dani ba. Se naji wani sanyi a raina, nifa Har mamakin hakan nake, gaba daya Ya shiga zuciyata da rayuwata Yayi min karan tsaye. 

"Tohm na bashi izinin neman aurenki, sannan na yaba dasu idan kin amince zan saka iyayenshi su zo ayi final magana. "

Daga Haka Ko sallama bamuyi ba ya kashe. Bin wayar nayi da kallo Bance komai ba se tsabar mamakin komai dake faruwa nake.

Na dade a tsaye a gurin tamkar an dasa ni, mamaki ne kwance akan fuskata na irin kokarin Abdallah, Ya daina nemana kenan ya zagaya yaje ya nemi aurena. Kaina na girgiza a raina Ina ayyana Dama tunda  hadu da Abdallah jikina ke bani akwai wani abu a tattare dashi game Dani. Ganin kafata ta sage yasa na janye jikina na koma gefen Nanah wadda har lokacin babu walwala a tare da ita. Dafata nayi nace 

"Nanah Karki manta abubuwan da na fada miki, wannan ba komai bane a soyayya, kada hakan yasa kiji kin karaya Ko Kin fasa, A'a soyayya Baa mata Haka, love is something that makes us complete beings, it destroys us and yet everyday we search for that. Kinga soyayya ita ce rayuwa, a person is not complete without it, so ki manta Deeda da qualms dinsa, a fara sabuwar Rayuwa. Inada muhimmin abu da zanyi Inshaaa Allah Anjima zan dawo."

Banma jira abinda zata cemin ba nayi gaba, inajin ina haki saboda doguwar Maganar da nayi babu Hutu, Ina shiga daki na kwanta rub da ciki ina nazarin rayuwata da yadda alamuran suke zuwa, a raina kullum hoping nake zanji Wasu na cigiyar yarsu, Baka me gashi, me doguwar fuska, Idanunta Manya, bakinta matsakaici, hancinta dogo irin na fulanin Adamawa, kafarta ta hagu bata da kyau tana limping dinta Wato dingisawa. To amma har yau ko sau daya ban taba jin irin wannan cigiyar ba, kullum na kwanta na tashi hope dina zanga iyayena amma Shiru, though nasan Allah kadai ya shirya min rayuwata shi yasan me Zai faru gobe. 

Hawayena na share da suka Fara min zarya, tashi nayi na zauna tareda daukar wayata na Fara neman layin Hamma Aiman, bai dauka ba Kamar koda yaushe katsewa yayi ya dawo da call din, Ina dauka yace

"Abdallah ya tafi Abdallah ya dawo, Iyeee kaga Amarya! "

Bata rai nayi hade da turo bakina nace 

"Harda kai aka hada Baki kenan Ko?"

Dariya yayi sosae yace

"Sister kenan, ni na isa Yanzun Mamaa ke fada min Wai Mama ta kirata tana fada mata"

Bance komai ba jin Har an Fara yamididi da Maganar, matsalar wide family kenan magana kadan zakaji ta bazu

"OK tell me, Ya kamarshi take? Kina sonshi? Kodan ya kamata nayi fushi tunda ban kai matsayin da zaki fada min ba. "

Ya karashe Maganar Kamar Wanda yaji haushi, duk se naji babu dadi a cikin raina se nake ga tamkar har Yanzun yana sona, naji Kamar na Faye son kaina da yawa da Naki saurarshi sede kullum ni ina Kai masa tawa damuwar ni ban taba sauraron tashi ba, balle naji Meye tashi damuwar. 

"Hamma Aiman, ka daina sona ne?"

Murmushi yayi yace 

"AI ban taba cewa ina sonki ba dama, na dai ce zan aureki saboda Mamaa Tana sonki sosae. All this while ina jinki Kamar ni kadai ne Wanda kike juyewa damuwar ziciyarki. Kamar ni kadai ne Dan uwan daya damu da damuwarki, ina fatan banyi hurting dinki ba."

Hawaye a hankali ya gangaro min Wanda koda wuka bansan na Meye ba, zuciyata tambaya ta tayi Dama Ina expecting yana sona ne ko kuwa? kaina na rausayar nace 

"If that's the case Nagode sosae Hamma, Allah Ya Kara zumunci "

Daga Haka na kashe wayar tareda kwanciya ina sakin sabon kuka. Sede Gabaki daya ji nake kamar something is missing, Kamar akwai abinda yake ba daidai ba, zuciyata se tuhumata take akan dalilin kukana, Dan yace baya sona to da yace yana sona me zanyi kenan, I'm to love him back. Gaba daya se na dawo inajin haushin kaina. Ina nan har aka kwalla kiran Maghrib, Kaina zuwa wannan lokacin Har ciwo yake ga idona da yayi ja saboda kuka da halinda nake jefa zuciyata, Wanda ni akaran Kaina banga dalilin hakan ba, watakila bansan Zaman lafiya ne. 

Ina idar da sallah naji wata azabbabiyar Yunwa tana damuna, Allah Ya taimaka nayi girki Tun safe hakan yasa na janyo flask din na saka tuwo da miya na wanko hannuna naci, naci sosae kuwa sannan nasha ruwa na jira akai ishai sannan na Fara shirin bacci. Seda na gama shirina tsaf sannan wayata ta Fara ringing, hannuna na Kai zan dauka na fasa ganin Abdallah ne me kiran, dauke kaina nayi na cigaba karkade katifar ina kokarin daidaita kaina amma ina hakan bai samu saboda yaki daina kirana ni kuma nutsuwata taki tsayawa in cigaba da abinda nake, cikeda masifa na dauki wayar tareda fadin 

"Menene Kake damuna? "

Tamkar baiji me nace ba yace

"Ki fito ina kofar apartment dinku Ina jiranki"

Bai Ko saurari ansa ta ba ya kashe wayar, bed sheets din na saki mamaki fal raina Ina ganin gadara, iko da kuma isa a gurinshi. Duk yadda naso kada na fita amma sam zuciyata taki bani Hadin Kai, Haka na dauki dogon hijab na fita, can gefe na sameshi shi daya yana ta faman danne danne a wayarshi Ya jingina da motarshi, yana sanye cikin kananan kaya Jeans coffee banga top din dake ciki ba saboda sweater dake saman kayan sede collar top dinshi ta samu damar lekowa ta wuyan, Yayi kyau Dama shidin me kyau ne, inda yake na isa tareda tsayawa a gefenshi ina wasa da yatsuna raina a hade nace 

"Ina yini? "

Dagowa yayi tareda tsura min idanun nashi masu sakani in duburburce, saurin kasa nayi da kaina, Ina kara tamke fuska. Murmushi yayi yace 

"Bakiyi kyau da shan kunu ba, gwara Ki saki fuskarki ko a samu kiyi kyau. "

Bakina na zunbura nace 

"Aini dama me kyau ce tuntuni, bana bukatar na saki fuska ko nayi dariya sannan aga kyauna. "

Ganin yadda nake bace bacen rai da kuma abinda na fada yasashi dariya wadda kular Dani kawai take, a ganina ya raina min hankali ne zai sani gaba yana dariya, gyara zamanshi Yayi tareda aje wayarshi akan bomber motar yace

"Da gaske? Kinga ban taba ganin kyan kiba hasalima nasan nafiki kyau"

Da sauri na dago idanuna Ina kallonshi, wata harara na wurga mishi nace

"Shiyasa Naga mutum ya dage dole seni, yana tsoron kar ya rasani ya tafi ta sama, yana ganin bai kai Yayi confronting dina ba"

Ina rufe baki naga ya bude nashi cikeda mamaki yace 

"Dama kina magana Har Haka? Wayace miki Tsoro naji? Kawai nabi abinda musulinci yace ne, banda abinki namiji Kamar ni ta Yaya zaiji tsoron mace. "

Dariya nayi jin yana kokarin kare kanshi yace 

"Duk ba wannan ba Hikmah, Kinada kyau sede gaskiya bashi yaja hankalina Kanki ba, I can't say ga abinda ya birgeni game dake kawai zuciyata na fada min kada na Bari ki tafi, ke din kika birgeni not looks ko wani abu, gaba dayanki nake so. Ummiey tace min inhar na aureki na gama saa, na aza ta fada ne saboda Kece best student dinta amma da naje gurin Baffanki naji babu wata yarinya data cancanci na aureta seke. Farin cikina Baffa yana sonki, ya yabeki to the core da har seda na Fara jin anya I'm I worthy enough na aureki? Inasonki Farin cikina"

Lumshe idanuna nayi a hankali inajin wata nutsuwa tana saukar min a dukkan jikina, wani abu ne ke fuzgar zuciyata, inajin Tana budewa wani sonshi yana shiga Ko ina. 

"Farin cikina"

Ya fada yana kallon yanayin da nake ciki, dagowa nayi nace 

"Baffa yace maka basu suka haifeni ba? Ba sune iyayena ba? "

Idanunsa a warwaje yace

"How comes kika sani? Waye ya fada miki? "

Wani faint smile nayi nace 

"Duniya da fadi, all my life sir ban taba kawowa cewar iyayena basu Baffa bane, bana tunanin ko iyayena zasui min wannan rikon da sukai min, basu taba bani wani gap da zai nuna min cewar basu suka haifeni ba amma dukda haka a gidan seda aka fada min matsayina aka cemin basu suka haifeni ba.... "

Se kuka ya kwace min, ni daya nasan ciwon da nake ji duk lokacinda na tuna hakan, na tuna rayuwata amma Haka Allah ya tsara, bai hanani seda nayi Dan isata amma jikinshi duk Yayi sanyi 

"...Ina jin tsoron kada ya zamana I was born illegitimate.... "

"Don't, don't ever Farin cikina, Ki kyautata zato. Tunda Baffa ya sanar damu Jikina ke bani tabbas kaddara ta rabaku, komai na ranar da suka tsince Ki seda ya nuna mana which shows ke din yar gata ce. Inshaaa Allah I'll be the reason da zakiga iyayenki. bana son ki Kara kuka, ki fawallawa Allah komai yana sane dake. "

Se naji dadi naji nutsuwa ta saukar min, shi ne mutum na biyu bayan Hamma Aiman da yasan yadda Zaiyi lecturing dina, ranar Har kusan Karfe goma muna tare dashi, kafin ya tafi seda ya sauke duk Wasu hadda dake Kaina seda ya barar da ita completely, Ya daura min sabon karatunshi Wanda ya tsaya min a raina, seda naji idan babu shi to Se rijiya..... Lol

*Russia*



"Like seriously yarinyar Kake jira Har tsawon wannan shekarun Maina, are you serious or you kidding me?"

Dr. Dalhat Ya tambayi Maina yana Kara sakin dariya Hade da tafa hannu, Maina dake zaune kawai kallonshi yake bai iya cewa komai ba, saboda shima yana ganin foolishness dinsa, ta Yaya zai zauna jiran abinda bai da masaniyar wanzuwar shi a duniya, it's been 20years kenan tunda ya Wayi gari Ammiey tace masa an sace Lulu, yana ta kuka ya yini baici Abinci ba, da sukaje Maiduguri Dada tace mishi ai zata dawo, duk inda take zata dawo, kodan wannan alkawarin auren da akai. Wannan auren zai dawo da ita gida shidai Ya dage kawai da addua. So Dada ita ta saka mishi wannan hope din, ita ta Kara dasa mishi wannan soyayyar tareda rike alkawari amma kwana biyunnan bukatunshi suna neman fin karfinshi, shekarunsa sunkai yadda yake bukatar opposite gender Dan Ya cika mutum kuma namiji, so har ya Fara tunanin Anya bazaiyi aure ba idan yaso duk lokacinda Allah yasa ta dawo gida Dan shi jikinshi na bashi she's coming, she'll come for him. To amma tambayar ita ce yaushe? Se yaushe zata dawo? Ta Yaya kuma? Bai sani ba, Allah shi ya barwa kanshi sani. 

"Abokina Ya zanyi? Nifa saboda yadda na sata a rai ko kallon Wasu matan banayi balle har naji Meye taste dina"

Wani kallo Dr. Dalhat yayi mishi sannan yace

"Seriously this is Delusional, ban taba tunanin zanji makamankciyar rayuwa irin taka ba Maina, how can you? "

Se kuma yayi Tsaki sannan yace

"See, wata ya rage kawai mu gama komai, ka manta da yarinyar kawai kayi aurenka, ina wannan yarinyar dake stalking dinka? Ka aureta mana amma babu yadda zaayi ka zauna jiran gawon shanu, what if idan Tazo ka ganta gurguwa ko a nakashe? Zaka sota still"

Se a sannan Maina Yayi dariya tareda buga kafadar Dalhat yace

"Mutumina kana sha'aninka, wayace maka zan sota ne? Ai zuciyata da sonta ta ginu kaga I'll accept her da duk yadda na ganta, as far as zata kauda Idanunta daga flaws dina bana tunanin akwai abinda zai gagara"

Kai Dalhat Ya Jinjina  tareda kurbar coffee din da tuntuni ya dade da yin sanyi, Dan tuntuni akai serving dinsu amma saboda they're heated up in a serious conversation shiyasa basu ma sha ba, seda ya shanye tas sannan ya aje tea mug din akan saucer yace 

"Kafi karfina Maina, kasa yarinyar da yawa a zuciyarka. Allah ubangiji ya zaba muku mafificin alkhairi. Amma kayi Trusting rayuwa da yawa"

Kai Maina Ya girgiza yace 

"Nagode sosae Abokina Kaima you have contributed so far. Amma Kasan ba rayuwa nayi trusting ba, Allah nayi trusting, nasan addua ta bazata taba tashi a banza ba. Kaidai mu hanzarta muje class"

Mikewa kawai sukai suka nufi inda zasuyi lecture din.

A hankali rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya, by each passing day zuciyata tana kara karbar Abdallah, tana kara nuna min shi din shi ne abinda ya cancanta Dani. Gaba daya Abdallah ya siye min zuciyata banida wani tunani idan ba nashi ba, kome zanyi se na Fara fada mishi ya bani shawara kafin nayi. A hankali har wata guda Ya tafi anan naje weekends Kano, munjima muna hira da Mama sannan nayi wanka naci Abinci nayi sallah na dasa waya. 

"Farin Cikina baki ganin ya dace su Papa suzo ayi formal magana, Nifa gwaurancin nan ya isheni haka"

Dan Murmushi nayi nace 

"Kai Tun Yanzun ka bari idan na gama level 3 se ayi Maganar kafin azo bikin kuma na gama school gaba daya"

Wata dariya ya saki yace

"Haba? Da gaske kike? Tabdijam! I can't wait gaskiya, Dan ni so nake nanda 4months ace kina gidana"

Idanuna na zaro tareda marairaice fuska nace

"Kai Sir...."

Cikin hanzari ya katseni da fadin 

"Farin Cikina, me yasa bazaki daina cemin Sir ba for crying out loud? Se kace wata dalibata. "

Toshe bakina nayi da tafin hannuna saboda kada nayi dariya nace 

"To da ni mecece, Inace har useless and stupid girl ka cemin"

Nasan ya tsana na tuna masa Farkon haduwarmu, Yanzun nan zai fara fada, amma ranar se naji Ya kashe wayar. Dariya na karayi na mike na fito parlor daidai shigowar Hamma Mussadiq, Karo na farko da na saki Murmushi Tun bayan haduwar mu a Mota 

"Hammana! Sannu da zuwa"

Shima murmushin Yayi yace 

"Hikmata yaushe kika dawo? "

Zamannan nayi gefen Mama tareda daura kaina akan kafadarta nace 

"Dazun nan, kuma Mama bata siya min kaza ba"

Tabe Baki Mama tayi ganin mun saki jiki da juna, da kuwa tamkar Baki haka muke. Tureni tayi yayinda Hamma yake ta dariya, Hakan yasa nace 

"Mama ki daina haka, kinga kina bawa su Hamma kofar yi mata dariya, Yanzun idan ban kwanta jikinki ba ina zani"

Dungure min kaina tayi tace 

"Dadin baki ko?"

Mikewa Hamma Yayi yana dariya yace 

"Grilled Ko roasted chicken kike so?"

Wani irin Murmushi na saki nace 

"Hammana yafi na kowa, grilled nake so, sannan inason yoghurt da strawberry cheesecake"

Kallona Yayi yace 

"Bafa Abdallah bane, Mussadiq ne"

Dariya nayi nace 

"To ta Yaya zance Abdallah ya siyo min duk wannan, saboda Kai dinne shiyasa. "

Ficewa yayi ni kuma na juya Kan Mama na fada mata sakon Abdallah, tana tamin tambayoyi akanshi sannan tace

"To Allah ya tabbatar muku da alkhairi, dama karshen satin nan zaa saka rana, Baffanki ke kadai yake jira. "

Ance idan akai zancen Zaa bada Kai, kuka kake amma ni wani ihun murna na saki, Mama tasa pillon gefenta ta kwada min tareda fadin

"Ke dai Anyi ja'ira, Kunyar nan ta Fulani ke kam bakida ita. "

A guje na wuce dakina a raina Ina fadin ta Yaya zanyi Kunyar Fulani bayan ni ba bafullatana bace. Dama ni tuntuni fullanci bai birgeni Dan Ko Yaren yinshi nake Dan dole Dan Ina cikinsu amma aladarsu gaba daya batai min ba, inda akwai culture da nake mugun so to barebari ne, sumin mutanen. 

Gefen gado na zauna tareda janyo wayata na rubutawa Abdallah 

_Galaxy, Mama tace next weekend Zaa saka mana official ranar bikinmu. I can't wait na zama mallakinka. I love you_

Ina gama rubutawa na tura mishi lokacin yana gaban Anty Talatu da Papa, Anty Talatun ita ce wadda Papa ya aura bayan rabuwar shi da Ummiey. Wayar na dakinshi bai san na turo ba. 

"Ai Prof sede ya hada ita Zainab din da Mupheeyda Ya aura Rana daya, tunda ni dai ban isa cewa Mupheeyda Abdallah ya fasa aurenta ba"

Da sauri Abdallah yace

"Nifa bani nace Ina sonta ba, Dan Haka babu uban Wanda ya isa Ya min auren dole a gidannan"

Wata tsawa Papa ya dakawa Abdallah yace

"Ubanka Babana, Ashe Bakada kirki, Talatun tana fada Kana mayar mata. Saarka ce? To tunda Hakane sede idan ka hakura da ita Zainab din amma dole ka auresu su biyu"

Wani murmushin nasara Anty Talatu tayi tana jijjiga kafa, Abdallah ranshi a matukar bace ya bude baki Zaiyi magana, Papa yace

"Bana son jin Wata magana kuma, ka dauka zancen nan an gama shi, ni zan fahimtar da iyayen Zainab din, saboda nasan kana sonta! "

Abdallah bakin ciki yasa ya Mike tareda zabgawa Anty Talatu harara ya nufi dakinshi, yana zuwa ya Fara zagaya dakin, shi dai tunda yake karan farko da yaji yana matukar son Abu a rayuwarshi, shi baya son aure saboda yana daukar aure as liability, tuntuni abinda ya hana Ya auri Mupheeyda kenan. To amma zuwan Hikmah yasa yaji aure nima ce da Allah Ya saukar tsakanin mace da namiji. To kuma ta Yaya zai aure su biyu Wanda kwatakwata babu wata hanya da kamanninsu Ya hadu Ko halayarsu. Yana kallon yadda Anty Talatu ke juya Papa inhar tayi magana, Papa bai isa Ya Musa ba, sannan kawai ya auri yarta shima a asirce shi ya zama kan tace? Kanshi Ya dafe yana jin yadda kan ke Sara mishi. 

Nikam nayi ta jiran reply dinsa amma har safe babu ko amsa balle kira, Tun anan nasan tabbas babu lafiya hakan yasa ina idar da Sallar asuba na Fara kiranshi amma wayarshi a kashe, gabaki daya se naji tsoro ya kamani, let it not be shima he's breaking up, kada ya zamana ba zama yazo yi ba. Haka nayi ta sakawa Ina kuncewa tareda hasaso anything possible da zai iya faruwa. Amma iya tunanina babu abinda yazo raina. Baccin da ban koma ba kenan, na shiga shirts kayana Dan inason zuwa da wuri naga halinda Abdallah ke ciki. 

Wajen azahar na tafi, gaba daya hankalina a matukar tashe, Hamma Mustapha Ya kaini Tasha na hau Mota Inata trying numbers dinshi amma amsar daya ce "It's switched off, try again later"

A hankali hawaye ya Fara sauka min daga idanuna, shikenan Haka rayuwata zata cigaba, Haka zan kasance kullum zuciyata Shattered, idan na tattara pieces din na hade Se wani ya kara shammata ta ya wargaza, rufe kaina nayi cikin hijab Inata kuka a Haka barawo ya lallabo Ya saceni nayi bacci! 

Ina tafiya da daddare masu neman aurena mawa Abdallah suka zo, wannan karan hadda Papa Wanda Yayi squeezing yazo, Ashe abokin Baffa ne Sunyi wani PgD tare. Se sabuwar gaisuwa da kuma hira Har Papa yaji Kunyar fada masa abinda ya kawoshi amma yazaiyi Haka ya mishi bayanin duk yadda ake ciki. Baffa yayi Shiru tareda Jinjina alamarin, can dai yace

"Babu yadda zaayi da hukuncin Allah, ina ganin mu barwa Allah zabi tunda shi ya kaddaro hakan. Se mu karba a matsayin kaddarasu. "

Papa cikeda farin ciki ya karakace ya zaro kudi ya aje gaban Baffa yace

"To ai Abu yayi kyau ina ganin a kammalla komai kawai. Wannan kudin 100k na tambaya, 150k na sadaki, Allah Ya tabbatar da alkhairi"

Daga Haka kowa Yayi addua, a take aka saka rana watanni biyu masu zuwa, babu Wanda ya tuna Dani, babu Wanda yace ya kamata aji opinion dina amma Haka aka zartar da hukunci. Seda Baffa ya dawo yake bawa Mama kudin tareda fadin 

"Bansan yadda zaki dauki Maganar ba, amma ni a gurina komai da sauki yazo. Baban Abdallah dinne yazo akan zancen saka rana. Mun tsaida watanni biyu Dan hankalina Zaifi kwanciya. Bana son Nesan nan da Uwata tayi damu, gwara idan tanada aure shikenan"

Cikeda farin ciki Mama tace

"Mashaaa Allah, jiya take cemin yace zasu turo Ashe shi bai sani ba suna hanya. To Allah ya sanya alkhairi. "

"Amin " Baffa ya amsa tareda gyara zama yace

"Akwai Dan damuwa, amma to me bawani abin bane, nasan Kema zaki fahimci hakan. Mahaifinshi yayi min bayani akan tuntuni akwai Agola Dayake riko a gidanshi, to Dama Yayi alkawarin zai aurawa shi Abdallah wannan yarinyar, shi ne yace sai Ya auresu su biyu"

Da sauri Mama tace

"Me kace musu? "

Shikanshi seda yayi shayin bata amsa saboda yadda ta tsareshi da ido, Hakanan yace 

"Nace musu duk tsarin Allah ne, babu damuwa"

Kai Mama ta dafe tana jin wani irin a kanta, me yasa maza suke haka ne. Ina Hikmah ina kishiya? Yarinya da bata san komai ba. 

"Haba Malam, Yaya zakai haka. Me yasa Bakace zaka tuntube ni ba, me yasa ka yanke hukunci Baka tuna Yaya Hikmah zata karbi zancen ba, kana ganin bazata ga kamar Dan bamu muka haifeta bane? "

Dan Tsaki ya saki, Dan shi ganinshi hakan ba komai bane, to Meye Dan Ya auresu su biyu? Shi Baiga wani abu ba. 

"To Menene a ciki Dan Ya auresu su biyu? Ni banga wani aibu ba. Hikmah yata ce, I trust her nasan bazata taba bani matsala ba. Kede ki kwantar mata da hankali sannan kiyo list a Fara shirye shirye, Dan zan aurar da yata, zanyi gaggarumin bikin da Baa taba yin irinshi cikin gidannan ba! 





Who saw This coming?

Bani na isa Zaria ba se gab da Maghrib, lokacinda na shiga dakina Kaina Kamar zai tsinke saboda azabar ciwo, ga wani palpitation daya sakani gaba, idanuna har dusudusu nake ganin wannan yasa nasan tabbas Bpna Ya hau. Bana sallah hakan yasa na kira Mama wadda tana zaune jugum ta rasa abinda ke Mata dadi gani take baayi min adalci ba, ta Yaya Baffa zai iya yin hakan, gani take an zalunce ni ne kawai. Tana cikin wannan yanayin Sega wayata tana shigowa, kasa dauka tayi dan gani take da zarar ta dauka zan fahimci halinda take ciki. Sau biyu ina kira bata dauka ba, hakan yasa na hakura na gwada number Abdallah, inajin ta shiga naji tamkar an tsoma ni cikin ruwa saboda yadda naji dadi a raina, amma har ta gama ringing Baa dauka ba. Kashe wayar nayi baki daya na kwanta tareda runtse idanuna, duk wani tunani daya taho min Se na kawar dashi saboda Ina bukatar adequate rest Ko na samu Bpn ya sauka. Cikin raina Inata maimaita Hasbunallhu wa niimal wakil! Cikin kamkanin lokaci naji komai ya Fara lafawa ban jima Ba bacci ya daukeni. Tunda na kwanta nake wani irin mafarki me tayar da hankali, a firgice na tashi Ina maimaita adduar Wanda Yayi mummunan mafarki Ko ya kasa bacci

_Audhu bikalmatillahi tammati, min gadabihi, wa iqamihi, Wamin sharri ibadihi, Wamin hamazati shayadin, wa an yahduru!_

Tashi nayi na kuuna standing fan sannan na tafi bayi nayo alwala, time na duba naga daf ake da Fara kiran Sallar asuba, hakan yasa na Fasa komawa kawai na hau sallaya Ina kaiwa Allah kukana. Seda nayi asuba sannan na duba timetable naga Se 9am nikeda class hakan yasa na koma na kwanta, bacci sosae nayi se takwas da yan mintuna sannan na tashi. Wanka nayi Nazo nadaura Abinci, kafin na gama na shirya na duba Bpna Naga ya sauka sannan naci Abinci.

Karfe Tara saura Minti biyar muna zaune nida Nanah, gaba dayanmu babu me magana Se Amjad ke ta janmu da hira ganin da gaske Shiru shirun muke ji yasa kawai ya rabu damu, amma ni bansan ya Nanah takeji ba, Nikam komai baya birgeni, lecture ma Dan dole na zota dan nasan Urinary physiology ba saan wasa na bane, if not da Bazan shigo ba. 

Muna nan zaune se bayan awa daya sannan class rep yayi making announcement cewar bazai samu damar zuwa ba, Ko Damuwa banyi ba, inajin yadda mutane ke zaginshi amma hankalina yayi gaba, so nake kawai naga Abdallah naji dalilin da yasa ya shareni for good 3 days. Ko su Nanah Ban jira ba na tafi office dinshi. Ko knocking banyi ba, Ina zuwa na tura kofar da karfina na shiga ciki, yana tsaye Ya zura hannayensa cikin jeans pocket dinshi, Wanda color ya zama pale brown, top din jikinshi milk ce me taushi, babu rubutun komai a jiki se tambarin designer din, fuskarshi tayi fayau, idanunshi sun fada ciki kamar Wanda Yayi jinya. Kallona yake kamar yadda na zuba mishi ido, dauke kanshi Yayi tareda fadin

"Zan sameka a office, se mu karasa maganar"

Se a lokacin na fahimci Ashe bashi daya bane, Juyawa nayi naga wani mutum Wanda sun kusa sa'oi da Abdallah Ahmad ban taba ganinshi ba. Murmushi yayi min sannan ya fice bayan Ya ja mana kofar. Zagayowa Abdallah yayi gabana ya tsaya kamar 3 feet away,

"Hikmah please sit"

Ya fada a hankali Kamar me rada, dagowa nayi dukda kwarjinin da yayi min amma seda na zabga mishi harara nace

"Kanada wannan bakin Dama? Abdallah me Kake nufi? is that treatment a bye bye? "

Kanshi Ya dafe yana girgizawa yace

"I've messed up Hikmah, kina tunanin cikin hayyacina zan iya cewa na fasa? duk ranar da aka ce miki Abdallah ya fasa aurenki, to kisa a ranki I'm insane, I'm possessed wallahi. Bazan taba yin hakan ba, Bazan taba cutar ki ba. Yanzun ki zauna muyi magana"

Yayi nasara Dan kalamansa Sunyi tasiri sosae cikin zuciyata, a hankali jikina a sanyaye na zauna inajin haushin Kaina da nayi tunanin cewar Abdallah zai iya rabuwa Dani, zai iya yaudarata. Se naji babu dadi. 

"Zainab its very hard Kinji, amma I got you farin Cikina. Ban taba fada miki ba saboda da farko ban taba kawowa cewar hakan zata iya faruwa ba. Kinsan VC shi ne Mahaifina, Mrs Hassanah Uba ita ce Ummiey ta. Sun rabu Tun kafin na gama secondary school, I taught may be zasu maida aurensu but basu mayar ba, kowannensu yaje Yayi aurenshi abinshi. I came from a broken family, shiyasa Bana son aure , a da ina ganin duk aure haka yake ayi a rabu, later na gane namu ne Haka, saboda kaddarar mu kenan. Papa yayi auri wata lecturer Hajiya Talatu, matar nan is cruel ban taba ganin azzaluma irin ta ba, seda ta maida Papa se abinda tace shi yakeyi. Tazo da yarinyar ta Mupheeyda wadda take completely opposite of her sede Kinsan Hausawa sunce barewa bata gudu danta yayi rarrafe, she's more poisonous than uwarta, shiyasa muka daina shiri, her only weakness is I, tana sona Hikma, so Wanda banda mugun halayenta da nayi dace. Anyi zancen Zamui aure na ki amincewa kuma daidai lokacin kika shigo rayuwata shiyasa na tabbatar kafin na shawo Kanki naje gidanku, naje komai ya tafi daidai, sede ranar da mukai waya last ina fitowa Papa ya kirani na sameshi parlonshi, tunda na Tarar da Anty Talatu zaune nasan akwai matsala, kafin naje ta gama mishi famfo akan sede na aureku ku biyu...... "

"Mu biyu kace Sir, nida wa kenan? "

Na katseshi da sauri ina ware masa idanuna, duk wannan kwarjinin Se naga babu ko daya, ban ma gani ba balle naji shayin abinda zan fada masa. Cikin sauri yace

"Hikmah Ki tsaya kiji Dan Allah "

Ya fada yana hade hannayesnshi guri guda alamun roko, kaina na kawar ,Har idanuna sun cicciko da kwalla nace

"Bazan iya ba Sir! Zuciyata bazata dauka ba, Wallahi Bazan iya ba. Me yasa Se ni, me yasa rayuwa ba tamin adalci, me yasa kowa yazo se ya fasa min zuciyata, seda aka gani na fara sonka sannan wannan stupidity zai zo? "

Mikewa nayi Ina jin yadda jikina yake Bari, Kamar zan fadi haka nakeji. Da sauri ya riko hannuna yana fadin

"Hikmah don't leave me, ya kikeso nayi? Ya zanyi? "

Kwace hannuna nayi cikin zafin Nama nace 

"Tambayata Kake ne? Ni kake tambaya ya Zakayi? "

Dariya nayi kuma kuka nake sannan nace

"Tun kafin na shiga kenan anamin zancen kiahiya, ina marainiya a salwantar min da rayuwa! A'a wallahi "

Daga Haka Ko sauraranshi banyi ba na wuce daga office din, hijabina naja na rufe fuskata Inata sauri na tafi daki, saboda yadda jikina ke karkarwa dakyar na bude dakin, na fada gado, zuciyata Kamar zata fito Haka nake ji. Nayi kuka na rashin sanin ya zanyi, nayi kuka na rashin Gata, na tahumi rayuwa akan walagigi da takeyi Dani, kamar zan haukace haka nakeji. Ina nan akai azahar Har laasar ban tashi ba, ba kuma ni da niyyar yin hakan. Ina kwance Kamar ruwa ya cinye ni, Inata kwance har maghariba sannan hankalina ya fara kwanciya kawai abinda na yanke hukunci shi ne na hakura da Abdallah, Dan Bazan iya kishi ba, Bazan iya masifa ba. 

Ina nan kwance na tashi zauna tareda Fara kiran number Mama, cikin saa ta dauka, yadda taji muryata a dashe yasa tasan na samu labarin abinda ke faruwa. 

"Mama ki cewa Baffa na fasa auren Abdallah"

Gabanta taji ya fadi tace

"Subhanallah, lafiya Hikmah?"

Hawayena na share nace

"Mama Wai mu biyu zai aura, nida wata kanwarshi, Mama ni Bazan iya ba, ina zan saka raina? "

Se na kara fashewa da kuka, Shiru tayi ita batace komai ba, ita bata lallasheni ba seda nayi Shiru sannan tace 

"Kiyi hakuri Hikmah, bansan me ya hau kan babanki ba, sun mishi bayanin da Abdallah yayi miki amma ya amince har ya karba sadaki Anyi fixing date nanda wata biyu"

Hawayen na share Kamar zan fashe Dan kuka, Baffa saida ni Zaiyi? At least ya dace a bani option tunda ni zan zauna dashi ba wani ba, ta Ina zanyi hakan, ta Yaya ai baamin adalci ba ko kadan ba? Kuka sosae na dinga yi inajin Mama tana rarrashina tareda bani baki, amma ko jinta banayi saboda ni banida hakuri bana daukar abu da sauki komai a gurina me zafi ne shiyasa nake shan wuya kuwa. Can Mama ta kashe baafi mintina goma ba Aiman Ya kirani, Bazan taba ganin call din Hamma Aiman naki dauka ba, hakan yasa na dauka na fada mishi abinda ke faruwa

"Hikmah wannan abin is simply, is just that Baffa ya rigada yayi complicating abin. Amma bakina addua ba?"

Kaina na gyada, ban bashi amsa ba yace

"Then, kiyi Shiru kiyi istikhara for good three days. Amma Se kin fitar da komai a ranki daga ranki, se Kinsa a ranki Allah Kika bawa Zabin. To kuwa Allah zai zaba miki. "

Ya jima yana min nasiha sannan mukai sallama, fuskata na shiga na wanke nayi wanka, na canja kaya sannan na rufe dakina na tafi wani clinic inda nake follow up, Doctor seda ya min fada saboda yadda Bpna yayi shooting, a Daren dai a clinic din na kwana. 

Wasawasa Abdallah ya dauka da gaske zan hakura na sakko amma har yayi kwana uku bana amsa call dinshi. Da daddare Ina kwance daki Sega Nanah ta shigo, Ko zama Batayi ba tace

"Sir Abdallah yana waje yace kizo."

Mikewa nayi saboda bansan further questions daga gurin Nanah shiyasa na fita, Ina Fita na ganshi cikin mota, gefenshi Umminshi ce kamar Ko yaushe Tasha gayu Har Daren ma Bazaa huta ba. Ina isowa dukkansu suka fito, gefenta na koma na gaisheta ta rike min hannu, shikam ko kallonshi banyi ba balle yasa ran zan kulashi, cikin wani Eatery muka shiga can VIP section, babu kowa muka zauna sannan Ummiey tace min

"Zainab, Kinyi jinya ne? "

Da sauri na girgiza kaina nace

"A'a Ummiey"

Kanta ta gyada sannan tace 

"Ni Zainab bansan me zance ba, ni mecece nasan pain din, nasan abinda kikeji, banyi niyyar saka baki cikin Maganar ba, amma Abdallah zai cinyeni indai banyi intervening ba. Nasan baayi miki adalci ba ace Zaa aureku ku biyu. Amma waye yake tsallake kaddararshi, wanene babu shi, komai Allah Ya tsarawa bawansa there's wisdom behind it, so kada Ki damu, ki nemi Zabin Allah bazakiyi dana sani ba. Kuma bawai na goyawa Abdallah baya bane amma Bashida laifi, ki tayashi yiwa mahaifinsa biyayya"

Daga Haka ta Mike tace

"Kui hakuri da junanku, sannan ku fito se kuyi magana a waje"

Jikinmu dukka a sanyaye muka fita, Mussaman ni, ji nake kamar zanyi me. Seda ta tafi sannan yazo kusa dani yace 

"Farin Cikina! Kiyi hakuri Kinji Yan Matana, kiyi hakuri"

Na tsinewa zuciyata yafi a irga da har son Abdallah ke Kara samun gurbi cikinta, naji haushi na kuma. Kasa bashi amsa nayi Illa kallonshi da nakeyi ina ganin how will life be without this being, Abdallah is my weakness. Da kyar yasahwo kaina na yadda naci Abinci, amma magana Ko sau daya ban iya mishi ba. Har kofar apartment ya rakani sannan ya tafi, na jima sosae sannan na koma ciki inajin kewarshi, wai ita zuciya Haka take da naci akan abinda take so. 

Ina shiga daki na dauki qurani na karanta sannan na dakko books dina na Fara Karatu Dan idan banyi wasa ba zanyi CO semester dinnan. Har 1am sannan nayi bacci. Washegari Cardiovascular physiology muke dashi kuma Se 3pm hakan yasa na karfafa jikina nayi aiki sosae nayi Karatu. Bayan azahar Baffa ya kirani, duk haushi ma nakeji amma Haka na dauka muka gaisa sannan yayimin bayanin da na riga na sani, bakin cikin se lokacin yake tambayata opinion dina ni kuwa nace a mayar musu da kudin na fasa auren. Baice min komai ba ya kashe wayar, so guilt was eating me, AI Baffa ko Bera yace na aura ya kamata na mishi Biyayya balle mutum Wanda ni nace Ina sonshi, Anya kuwa ma nufin yar halak ce da zan ki musu Biyayya saboda son raina? Jikina Yayi bala'in sanyi haka na tattara na tafi class. 

Ban jima da zama ba class din ya cika Dan missing din lecture Abdallah is like playing with a Dragon, Bashida kirki when it comes to that, he always says bazai bata time dinshi making lecture note and some useless people will just stay home, Wanda bai zo ba ya san Sauran. 

Ina chatting da Najaatu wadda kemin jaje tareda dannar kirjin abinda ya faru, naji an buga desk din Dana daura kaina akai, a hankali na dago Kaina, nayi toxali da irin yan matannan ajebos, kyakyawa ta gasken gaske, fara ce kyakywa, tasa red lipstick Wanda Yayi mata kyau tareda haska fuskarta, tayi rolling da bakin veil hannunta na karkada car key, 

"Kece Zainab? "

Ta tambaya tana taunar chewing gum Tareda yin kas kas, kallonta nayi nace 

"Eh! How may I help you? "

Tsirtar da yawu tayi tana bina da kallon wulakanci tace

"Allah Ya isa amma! Ashe Kece Dama yake ta hankilo akanki, Kece Zaa hadamu kishi dake. Oh my gosh! Wannan abar"

Ta fada tana nuna ni, hade damin kallon wulakanci, bakina kasa budewa Yayi, se kallonta nake Dan na kasa figuring ita din Wacece, seda tace

"Nasan kina tunanin wacece wannan, sunana Mupheeyda, mace daya mallakin Abdallah Ahmad Abdallah, kin gane. You look innocent bana son kikawo Kanki makabarta, kiji da gurguwar kafarki..... "

Anzo gurin, Babu wani da ya isa tanka halitta ta na kyaleshi

"Karki! Karki fara, Useless stuff wadda bata san kanta ba, idan da kin yadda ke Kece I don't think zaki bata ma Kanki lokacin nemana, gurguwa ki rubuta ki aje ita ce kishiyarki, ita kadai a Zuciyar mijinki kuwa"

Dariya tayi dukda maganata ta mata ciwo kamar yadda tata tayi min zafi, cikin daga murya ga class din Anyi Shiru kowa hankalinshi akanmu, mutane including Nanah da Amjad yaushe muka hade da Abdallah ne? 

"Ashe zanyi yawo tsirara kenan, ashe ma ba Mupheeyda bace yar Talatu. Ki rubuta ki aje matsawar kika auri Abdallah, kika zama kishiyata to ki sani na haukace ko kuma na mutu."

Dariya nayi dukda gabana ya fadi nacr

"Gado ba karanbani ba. "

Har ta juya taji na fadi haka, Aikuwa tace

"Dadinta ni nasan asalina, nasan waye ya haifeni, ba Shegiya wadda aka haifeta a titi ba kuma aka yarda ita a titi"

Da sauri na mike zuciyata Kamar zata fashe saboda yadda take wani irin bugawa

Dau!!! Naji saukar Mari..............

Da sauri na saka hannuna na dafe kuncina dan dukkan tunanina ni aka mara, se kuma Banji zafi ba hakan yasa na waigo kan Mupheeyda wadda tuni hawaye sun bata saman kyakyawar fuskar da ake takama da ita, Abdallah ne a gabanta, idanunshi Sunyi jawur saboda bacin rai, ban taba ganinshi a Haka ba tunda nake. 

"Get out! "

Ya fada da karfi yana nuna mata hanyar waje, muryarta na bala'in rawa tace 

"Yaya Abdallah, ni ka mara? "

Hannunshi ya Kara dagawa zai sauke mata wani, nayi saurin rike hannun tareda girgiza mishi Kaina, itakam tama sadakar zata sha Mari Har ta runtse idonta. 

"Get out nace"

Ya kara maimaitawa, bude idonta tayi tareda min wani mugun kallo sannan ta gyada kanta tayi gaba, Binta yayi a baya har suka fita, se lokacin na sauke Ajiyar zuciya tareda dafe Kaina Wanda ya Fara min ciwo, wayata na dauka batareda nace komai ba nayi hanyar fita Dan Ko na zauna I'll understand nothing. Nazo tsakiyar hall din shima yana shigowa, turus na tsaya Har ya karaso inda nake, murya can kasa kasa yace 

"Farin Cikina, are you hurt? "

Dan Murmushi nayi na girgiza kaina nace 

"I'm ok as you can see"

Ajiyar zuciya me nauyi wadda har seda na kalleshi ya sauke sannan yace

Tea Party shi ne event na farko da muka farayi, shi ne ya kasance ranar Wednesday. Tun safe aka aiko min da outfit dina, Wata Kimolly gown ce ta wani rantsatsan Silver material, kasanshi black sequence ne. Ga komai accessories dina black. Ajewa nayi bayan na gama yabawa, a ranar akai min kunshina Yayi kyau Dan dawowata kenan na samu sakonshi. Karfen biyar na yamma muka tafi wajen make up wanda Yayi booking seda aka gama Allah Ya taimakeni period dina da safe yazo, Dama na dade ina wannan adduar yadda Bazan Je Ina missing sallah ba Har a kwashe albarkar auren ba. 

Misalin Karfe Takwas yazo daukata, friends dina Wanda rabinsu duk Yan ABU ne sunsha English gown dinsu, Sunyi kyau sosae abinsu. Sun fitar Dani kunya. Yana tsaye a jingine jikin wata black Benz, yana sanye da Grey tuxedo suit, yayi kyau naji tamkar na rungumeshi na nunawa duniya yadda nake jinshi a zuciyata. Tunda muka hada ido yake min Murmushi, haka na samu Kaina da Murmushi Wanda nakejin tamkar bakina zai yage saboda Murna. Shi ya bude min kofar na shiga, anan idanuna ya sauka akan Mupheeyda wadda ta hakimce ta baje. Tayi kyau babu karya kuma outfit dinta sak irin nawa, just that nata Baki ne sequence din ash. Sau daya ta kalleni ta maida kanta Kan wayar da take dannawa, seda na zauna sannan ya rufe ni ya koma gaba ya zauna, Umar ke driving. A ka'ida Wanda ya shiga guri shike sallama, nayi bata amsa min ba balle mu gaisa hakan yasa kowacce ta ja mutuncinta har mukaje wani Chinese restaurant anan akai Tea Party din saboda reserving gurin akai gaba daya. Mun sha hotuna, duk Wanda yazo daukar mu se nayi dariya because I was so and super excited, inajin Kamar wannan auren shi ne mabudin dukkan Wasu alkhairin rayuwata, dukda idan na Kalli Mupheeyda zuciyata bugawa take amma Ban Bari hakan Yayi clouding zuciyata ba, abinda ya zama dole Meye naka na fighting dinshi tamkar kana fighting da will din Allah kenan.

Ana kammalawa muka fito, I had lots of fun, abokansa are entertainers, sun bamu dariya sun sani farin ciki sosae. A cikin mota yace

"Hik zanyi dropping Mupheeyda ta kwana gurinki kafin zuwa gobe mu wuce kaduna"

Kafin na bashi amsa tace 

"No, na rigada nayi booking hotel room, I'm spending the night there "

Baice komai ba muka cigaba da tafiya, seda muka ajeta a hotel din sannan muka tafi, shike driving ina gefenshi yana nuna min yadda wannan satin yakeda muhimmanci cikin rayuwa, a wannan lokacin Yayi min alkawari Wanda inhar da komai ya tabbata bana tunanin zanyi kunci a rayuwata, but Allah is the best and the most perfect planner. A Wasu lokutan idan ya hukunta abu a kanmu mukanji tamkar inama ba haka abin yake ba, kanajin dama Haka akai, amma later on se ka fahimci there's reason and wisdom behind every will of Allah. Hakace ta faru dani. 

Muna shiga gida naje na cire kayana nayi wanka sannan Nazo gaban window na tsaya, a hankali inajin yadda iska ke daddaga curtains din, Garin Yayi dadi ga damuna da bata gama sauka ba, inajin yan mata nata hayaniya kowa yana neman gurin da zai kwanta amma Ni tunani daya ke min yawo a raina, Friday Zaa daura min aure, ranar lahadi zan cika shekaru ashirin da uku a duniya, daidai shekaru ashirin da daya Dana aka sace ni kamar yadda Hamma Aiman yayi min bayani. I so long to see them, inason naji Yaya 'Ya'ya sukeji gameda iyayensu, ban rasa komai ba amma duk lokacinda nayi sujadda Se nayi adduar reunion dinmu dasu. 

Nayi zurfi sosae cikin tunani naji hannu ya tabani lokacin ruwan Sama me karfi ya Fara sauka, feshin shi ya bata min fuska da kayan dake jikina. Mama ce hakan yasa na kirkiro Murmushi nace 

"Mama"

Kallona Tayi tareda rungumeni tace

"Zanyi kewar ki diyata, Allah Yayi miki albarka"

"Amin "

Na amsa inajin idanuna suna cikowa da kwalla. Dakin Hamma Mus'ab mukaje babu kowa a ciki, gefenta ta zaunar dani ta Fara min karatun aure

"Zainab! Kinsan Allah yace idan kayi aure mutum ya cika Rabin addininshi? Bakiyi mamaki ba? Ko daga wannan fadin Ya kamata kisan cewar aure abune me matukar girma, abune da yake bukatar juriya, hakuri da kuma dangana. Hikmah nasan bazaki bani kunya ba amma a matsayina ta mahaifiyar ki Yau Zan canja akalar yadda iyaye sukewa yayansu fada. Ni Uwa ce, ni mata ce nasan yadda aure yake, Abu ne me wuya Mussaman idan ka fada mummunan hannu. Muna cikin wata musiba ta yadda mata ke kashe mazajensu, saboda sun kaisu karshe bazasu iya jurewa ba, shiyasa nace miki mun yawaita a wuta, ga azabar miji a duniya gata Allah a lahira. Nasha ayyana cewar kamata yayi ace maza su suka yawaita a wuta ba mata ba, saboda su ke zaluntar mu, suna cutarmu, maimakon mu barsu da Allah Se mu saida imanin mu wajen bokaye, har takai ga kisa. A Yau nace miki idan Har kikaji bazaki iya hakuri akan mijinki ba ki dawo gida, Ki dawo mu zauna, da kije Ki tabe gwara ace kin kasa zaman aure. Ina miki addua ina miki fatan alkhairi a rayuwarki, Ki sani duk lokacinda kika kasa sarrafa Kanki, kikaji ya zakiyi ki tuna ni, zan zame miki kunnen da zai saurari kukanki. Se Abu na karshe nasan Kinada hakuri amma bakya daukar abu da sauki, komai a gurinki me zafi ne shiyasa kike shan wuya.... "

In takaice muku ban taba jin nasiha irin wannan ba, kullum abinda ake nuna mana, dole kiyi zaman aure koda kasheki miji keyi, we're in the era of female gender stigma, komai mukai we're blamed, Baa duba me maza suke sede mata. Kinyi aure biyu Ance ta kasa zaman aure, se yayi aure goma yaje neman na sha daya a bashi, mijinki Ya mutu after iddah kince zakiyi aure, society bata duba sexual need dinki ba tace da yaushe mijin ya mutu? Shi bayan sati daya da rasuwar mata yace Zaiyi aure, society tayi backing dinshi. Kin kasa iya girki, Baki Shara Ance saketa ka auri wata, ko kuma ka Kara aure, kin kamashi red handed da mace on bed, kin nemi saki Ance kin kasa rufa asirin mijinki. We need to change, ke da kika fadi haka Kema mace ce, muma hallita ne, For me a woman is the most easy going creature in the universe, but if you mismanage her, you go see shege.

Tare muka kwana da ita, cikin dare na kalleta na shafata naji wani sanyi a raina, Allah shi ne abin godiya. Washegari mutane suka zo sosae musamman na Nesa, gidan Ya cika sosae, kawayena Wanda basu zo ba yawanci duk sun karaso. Ranar akai kamu a FABS dake Magajin rumfa. Lord nayi kyau, kamu akai na gargajiya hade da aladar mu ta Fulani, babu namiji Dan Ango Tun gurin me make up mukai hoto. Na dawo gida a matukar gajiye, babu abinda nake son nayi kamar bacci amma ina Abdallah bai bada wannan damar ba, bamu taba hira so much ba Kamar ranar, dukkan abinda yakeso da baya so seda yayi min narrating haka zalika Nima, a wannan Daren muka daura alkawarrika da Dama a rayuwarmu. Munyi alkawarin komai runtsi komai wuya muna mugun tare amma ina Allah yafi mu sanin gobe! 

Washegari jumaa, jumaar da Bazan taba Mantawa ba, jumaar da komai ya Fara, Ya lalace ya kuma gyaru. *The Blessed Friday* Haka takenta yake a gurina. Tun safe masu Jere suka tafi Zaria, Tun safe ake gyaran jiki, kuma tunda aka Fara muke waya da Abdallah, na tsumu babu karya. Munata waya Har sha daya tayi nace yaje ya tafi Yayi wanka ya tafi massallaci kada yayi missing sallah Dan daurin auren Karfe uku ne na Yamma a Babban Masallacin Ibn Uthman Sahaba dake kundila. Daga nan mukai sallama, can wajen Karfe biyu na gama shiri cikin wani farin rantsatsan lace Yayi min kyau abinka da bakar mace, fuskata all dolled up. Nayi kyau anata hotuna Dani wayata Nanah ta mikon tareda fadin 

"Ango na kira"

Ganin sunanshi yasa gabana Yayi wani irin bugawa, se naji Kamar ana zare min duk wani kuzarina amma a hakan na kara a Kunne nace

"Galaxy"

"Awnnnnnn same"

Har ya kashe ya Kara kira yace 

"Ki sani whatever the outcome ina sonki, Ina kaunar ki and I longed to have you as my wife"

Bai jira nace komai ba ya kashe wayar, se naji tamkar ya tafi da dukkan wani Sauran Hikma yabar Zainab a tsaye. Yadda nayi Shiru yasa muka koma ciki na wuce dakin Hamma Mus'ab na kwanta ba tareda sanin kowa ba, inajin yadda jikina ke rawa gabana nata faduwa, komai was beyond na fada. 

Karfe uku dukkan Wanda ya kamata yaje gurin daurin auren ya hallara, banda Ango da waliyyansa. Tun ana jira cikin dadin rai Har mutane suka Fara kosawa, Baffa nata duba agogon hannunshi Tareda kallon kofa with the hope cewar Ango zasu shigo amma ina babu shi babu dalilinsu. Har hankalin mutane ya Fara tashi. 

"Baffa ka kira Mahaifinshi kaji mana, mutane nada nasu uzurin munzo mun tsaidasu, AI wannan wulakanci ne"

Baffa tuni ya cire babbar riga ya mikawa Hamma Mus'ab, wayarshi ya ciro Sega kiran VC yana shigowa hannunshi har karkarwa yake wajen picking call din, sede baice komai ba wayar ta zame daga kunnenshi ta fadi ta tarwatse, banda Innalillahi wa Inna ilahil rajiun! Babu abinda yake maimaitawa, Aiman ne ya taho yana fadin 

"Baffa me yace? "

Lumshe idonshi Yayi sannan ya bude tuni sun kada Sunyi jawur yace

"Sunce idan ta samu miji tayi aure"

Dif gurin yayi, atmosphere very tensed, Shiru bansan saukar numfashi, karar AC da fans babu komai. Bayan wajen 2 minutes gurin Ya kaure da hayaniya a take Hamma Mussadiq yace 

"Baffa a daura mana aure da ita!"

Hafsa na zaune parlon Mom Tana shan Tea dukda zafin da ake amma ta maidashi routine, a cewarta gwara ta dinga shan hot Tea after kowanne meal ko ta samu tadan rage kibar da takeyi. "Allah Ya isa dai ta rago ce"

"Inlaw zo kiga wani Tea Party, wani yaro ya auri mata biyu Rana daya."

Kafin Hafsa ta karba wayar taji ihun Nasiba yarinyar Ammar tana ihun 

"Yaya Maina oyoyo!"

Da sauri Hafsa ta Mike tayi waje yayinda Salima ta sake baki Tana tunani, a ranta ta Ayyana Dama abincin Maina ne Hafsa ke hanata dauka, no wonder! Daga kofar bedroom din ta tsura mishi idanu tana ganin yadda yaron nata ke Kara girma, shekaru suna Hawa kanshi, a ranta ta ayyana inama yana da aure he would have been completely a man. Hannunta taji ya riko fuskarshi a sake yace

"Ammiey ta, surprise right? "

Bayanshi ta buga tace 

"Really, banyi tunanin dawowarka ba, no wonder waccan wicked witch din keta aiki Tun safe "

"Mom yimin massage"

Ture kanshi Tayi tace

"Uwarka Hafsa ita zata maka massage, kaga da kayi aure basai kayi requesting massage ba. Da kanta za tayi maka"

Mikewa yayi yana dariya yace

"Ma, lemme take a shower, Nazo naci tasty dishes dinki"

Kanta ta gyada ya fice, seda ya leka Dada, Ummiey da Anty Yana sannan ya wuce yayi wankan. 

Dukkansu suna parlor banda jikokin se Maina shi daya acan gefe yana kwasar Abinci. Hafsa na zaune a tare, kalla wayarta tayi tace 

"Takwas Har tayi amma har Yanzun mutanen Kano Shiru"

Se lokacin Ummiey tace

"Aikuwa dai Maman Maina, Bari na kira Alhaji naji lafiya"

Amina matar Mubarak tace 

"Kede Kice Hubby bai dawo ba, hankali yayi gaba"

Yadda ta fada kasakasa Hafsa da Salima kadai suka ji, kafin Hafsa ta bata martani se sukaji sallamar su Alhaji, cikin rada Hafsa tace da Salima 

"In-law sallamar Yayanki tasa zuciyata melting"

Tsaki Salima tayi tace 

"ke kam kin shiga uku"

Dariya sukai anata gaisawa tareda musu barka da dawowa, Dada da Hafsa suka kwaso Abinci aka jera kan dining mat dake malale a kasa, duk lokacinda aka hadu da yawa to Tare suke cin Abinci, dama Sunyi sallah kafin su shigo hakan yasa suka ci Abinci, suka gama aka daura da hira, Alhaji Nafi'u ya Kalli Hafsa yace

"ke da tuni An kashe Bakinki, Yau Aminu Yayi aure"

A zabure Hafsa tace

"kai Yaya Nafi'u wannan ba wasa bane wallahi, Alhaji kana ji ko? "

Ta fada tana bata rai, kowa seda yayi dariya a parlon Alhajin sama yace

"Hafsa kira min Danki, we have an issue dashi"

Mikewa tayi ta nufi dakin Maina, yana zaune kan Sofa Ya lumshe idonshi, yana jin sallamarta ya bude idanunshi yace 

"MA"

Mikewa yayi yabi bayanta yana ta bata labarin Russia, Mussaman sanyin Garin, suna shiga parlon ya samu guri ya zauna bayan Ya gaishesu, kowa na tambayar yadda karatun ya kasance tareda fatan samun nasara. 

"Yau Kamar yadda kuka sani naje Kano, sede zuciyata ta tabu Munyi wani abu Wanda bansan yazan kirashi ba amma dai nasan will din Allah ne. Bayan Munje gaisuwa, muka tafi Sahaba masjid dan kaiwa Abokina ziyara, muna zaune yake ce mana Yanzun Zaa daura aure gurin, kafin wani lokaci an taru Dan daura auren, muna ciki a zaune, bansan me ya zaunar damu ba amma nafi tunanin rabo ne, Allah Yayi rabonmu ce"

Gyara zama Hafsa tayi Dan Bata fahimtar abinda Alhaji ke fada, Meye hadinsu da wannan Maganar

Ummiey ta fada, gaba daya parlon kowa Yayi Shiru kada Maina yaji labari, Dan Haka kurum yake jin Wasu irin uneasiness na mamaye shi , tareda faduwar gaba da baisan ta mecece ba. 

".....Abu biyu na tuna yadda mahaiyarta da kuma yarinyar, bayan nazarin da nayi na bada sadaki aka daura auren da jikana da nake jin na isa dashi aurenta. Wato kai Ibrahim Maina"

Kowa seda ya firgita tareda kallon Alhaji Mussaman matan, Maina Yayi Shiru Yana jin wani abu yana mishi yawo cikin jikinshi, tamkar an zare mishi laka haka yaji baya tareda kowanne guzari, kasa cewa komai Yayi Se istigfari da yake yi saboda baisan ya ayyana cewar Baa kyauta masa ba. "Kar na sake naji Bakinki, aure an rigada an daura, Babanta yace a bashi sati daya Zaiyi magana Se Kuje Ku ganta. But gaba dayanmu munji tamkar abinmu muka karbo. Maina Allah Yayi maka albarka"

"Amin"

Ya amsa kanshi a kasa, addua akai kowanne da nashi tunanin Haka kowa ya nemi makwanci, duk yadda Hafsa taso ganin Maina hakan bai yiwu ba saboda Alhaji Bukar na tsaye seda Hafsa ta tafi sannan ya tafi, ita tayi farin ciki ba kuma opposing zatai ba Kamar yadda suke tunani A'a, he surely needs someone Dazai lallashe shi amma tana shiga daki ta kirashi, yana dauka tace

Murmushi Hafsa tayi tana shafa kanshi tace 

"Relax Dear, Bari na hado maka tea"

Kai ya gyada mata, ita kuma ta fice daga cikin dakin. 


Bangarena tunda na shiga side din Hamma Mus'ab ban fito ba, sede lokaci zuwa lokaci Ina duba wayata ko text din Abdallah zai shigo cewar na zama matarshi Kamar yadda nake karantawa cikin novels. Amma har hudu tayi babu wannan text Ko kira, 
A hankali naji tsoro ya Fara shigata saboda gidan ba Kamar gidan biki ba, is too quite than usua. Cikin gida Mama ana ta nemana ni kuwa Ina kwance Abuna kuma babu Wanda Yayi tunanin kirana. Har Karfe biyar babu wani Amo bare labari, nasan dole idan aka daura auren ai zaixo muyi hoto, to Kodai Abdallah ya mutu ne? Ko accident Yayi? Na ayyana hakan, babu shiri Na tashi daga kwance da nake na fito parlor, nan mukai kicibis da Hamma Mus'ab, ko a fuskarshi zakaga yana cikin damuwa amma ina ban lura ba saboda banma niyyar masa magana ba seda ya riko hannuna sannan na dago na kalleshi, hijabi ya Mika min yace

"Saka "

Can gefe ya bude motarshi seda na shiga sannan ya zagayo ya shiga tareda fuzgar motar Yayi gidanshi, tare muka shiga ciki yace

Menene matsayina, runtse idanuna nayi tareda kwantar da kaina a saman cushion ina jin yadda yanayin jikina ya canja. Bansan mintina nawa nayi a Haka ba se naji sallamar duk Yan gidanmu, Baffa, Mama, Hamma Musbahu, Hamma Mussadiq, Hamma Mustapha da Hamma Mus'ab. Se kannen Baffa su uku. *Thank you so much Yaah kaagu... Oum Naeeym, she just called and told me ina shigowa kano na mata waya, grilled chicken and milk shake na jirana, saboda na aurawa Maina HIKMAH. Oh Allah I felt loved, Nagode sosae. I can't wait to see Friday..... Kwadayi tinx*



A wannan lokacin ji nayi kamar na kashe Kaina saboda wani irin bakin ciki da ya turnuke min zuciyata, da ace an barni na kadaice wallahi ko ban kashe Kaina ba se nayi attempting suicide. Gaba daya rayuwar baya Hamma Mus'ab yayi ta ne akan cutar dani amma wannan abin da yayi min shi ya wanke dukkan laifufukansa. 

Baffa dasu Hamma Sunyi iya bakin kokarinsu ganin sun bani baki, jinsu kawai nakeyi Dan jikina na fada min bayin kan Abdallah bane, no way Abdallah yana hayyacinsa zai ce bazai aureni ba, wannan Maganar hankalina bazai dauka ba. Naso ace da Abdallah yace ya Fasa Baffa baimin auren dole ba Naso ace kyaleni yayi na samu wani amma tunanin yadda zanyi coping da zama da Wanda bana so shike kara sani kuka. 

Kowa ya tafi ya rage daga ni se Nanah se Aneeysa wadda mijinta ya dawo da ita se kuma Najaatu. Tun tuni na cire kayan ina zaune kan sallaya na idar da Sallar ishai, kaina cikin cinyoyina ni ba kuka nake ba amma gwara ace kukan nake. Gashi a lokacin nasan tuni BP na ya hau sboda dukkan Wasu signs sun bayyana. Wayata dake hannun Nanah ce ta Fara ringing, 

_Nothing is gonna change my love for you, you ought to know by now how much I love you, one thing ......._

Ko bata miko min ba nasan Abdallah ne kadai yakeda wannan ringtone a cikin contact dina. Katse kiran Nanah tayi hakan yasa na dago da sauri, dukkansu kallona suke nace cikin dasashiyar murya

"Nanah, Ya kika katse?"

"Me zai ce miki kuma, bakuda alakar da ta rage"

Kaina na girgiza hawaye na sauka a hankali nace

"We have Nanah, baimin bayani ba, bansan dalilinshi ba. Nima kukan nan da nakeyi ba so nakeyi ba, na gaji dashi....."

Ban karasa ba naji Ya sake kira, cikin sauri na karba nayi swiping tareda amsawa

" _Farin cikina_ Ban cika alkawarin da na dauka miki ba, I failed you, I didn't fulfilled promises din da nayi miki. Da nasan Haka zamu kare da ban kulaki ba, da banyi breaking miki zuciyarki ba, duk yadda bansan ganin kukanki amma Ni ne sanadin kukan..... "


Tuni na fashe da wani irin kuka, zuciyata gaba daya ta narke, bai hanani ba seda nayi me isata sannan nace

"Just tell, from the start dama abinda Kake nufi Dani? Dama duk alkawarrikan daka dauka ba Har zuciya suke ba? Abdallah why will you do this, why will you?..... "

Ban karasa ba wani kukan ya Kara kubce min, nayi kuka sosae shima bai hanani ba, seda nayi me isata kafin yace min 

"Kiyi hakuri Hikmah, nasan a gurinki I'm a monster, nasan na cancanci dukkan Wasu sunaye da zaki kirani dashi, amma abu daya nake so dake Dan Allah ki saurareni"

"Bayan mun gama waya, nace miki na dawo daga massallaci. Straight hotel din da muka sauka da Abbu nan mukaje, tuntuni shi ya shirya nima dakina na shiga na shirya. Abinda zan tuna Abbu yace min muje kada mu makara, daga nan na yanke jiki na fadi, Nidai consciousness dina bai dawo ba seda akai Sallar Maghrib daf da ishai. A gigice na mike ganin duhu Ya shiga, na kalla mutanen gurin, Abbu, Ummiey, kannen Abbu biyu da kannen Ummiey, Idanunta Sunyi jawur alamar Tasha kuka, hannunta na kama ina kuka nace, Ummiey An daura auren? Na aura hikman? Gaba daya Shiru sukai suna kallona se Abbu ne ya cemin, Abdallah cewa kayi Baka sonta ka fasa aurenta, cewa kayi daka aureta gwara ka mutu, I wanted to force you aurenta amma yadda kayi baimin dadi ba, Abdallah haka na kira mahaifinta nace a aura mata wani, a take kuwa Yayi mata miji, ka dauka haka Allah Ya tsara maka. Hikmah kuka kawai nake Dan nasan I was possessed, bayin kaina bane...... "

Gaba daya seda tsikar jikina ta tashi saboda yadda yake kuka, Nima Haka na shiga yin nawa bazance asiri bane kai tsaye amma abin da mugun mamaki, all of a sudden. 

"Ki yafe min, ki sani ina sonki Bazan taba Mantawa dake ba. Ashe we're not meant to be, dama wani baya aurar matar wani. Inasonki Ina kaunar ki Hikmah, Naso aurenki na rayu dake amma Allah bai nufa ba, ke matar wani ce. "

Munjima muna hira dukda inner self dina na fadamin babu kyau hakan amma naji hankalina ya kwanta sosae, at least naji sanyi saboda nasan ba yin kanshi bane, na yadda dashi Haka nan naji hankalina ya kwanta. Seda na daina kuka Har ina Murmushi sannan Yayi min adduoi tareda fatan alkhairi, Naso sosae na Tambayeshi Mupheeyda amma nasan ba hurumina bane. 

Ina aje wayar naji wata irin nutsuwa da dangana Ya shiga zuciyata ban taba tunanin hankalina zai kwanta ba Kamar haka. Mikewa nayi daidai shigowar Hamma Mus'ab, Da murnarshi yace

"Hikmah, Alhamdulillah! "

"Zauna kici"

Komawa nayi na zauna dukda banida appetite amma Haka na bude ledar, shawarma ce da yoghurt, kallonshi nayi Ina Murmushi na tuna Marin da nasha akan wannan abin. Kaina na girgiza Ina tuna rayuwa, Surely no condition is permanent in life! Seda naci sannan nace

"Hamma waye aka aura min? "

"Bansanshi ba amma nasan yanada nasaba me kyau, sunanshi Ibrahim, Likita ne. Asalinsu Yan maiduguri ne. Shine kawai abinda na sani game dashi. Kinyi saa shi ne abinda kawai zance miki, Inshaaa Allah bazakiyi regretting ba. "

"Jiya Bakiyi bacci ba, kije Ki kwanta ko. "

Washegari shi ne birthday Dina, nayi bacci bayan Sallar asuba bani na tashi ba se goma shima saboda Nanah zata tafi ne, Har mamakin yadda na dangana nake babu attack balle BP rise. "Hikmah ina zakije? "
